<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The UI then offers a preset picker <em>and</em> a custom mode that exposes each knob individually.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Impact on what's already been built</strong></p>
<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">types.ts</code> — needs <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> added, minor additions otherwise</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">board.ts</code> — <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">getLegalMoves</code> and <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">applyMove</code> need to accept and branch on <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code>. This is the bulk of the rewrite. The current logic needs to be refactored before any more is layered on top.</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">AI service — the eval function and search also need <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> awareness (e.g. flying king changes piece value weights significantly)</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">API / DB — <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> gets stored on the game document and passed in every AI request</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Visual debugger — should get a RuleSet selector so you can test the same position under different rules</li>
</ul>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Recommendation</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Update the PRD before Phase 2 starts. Specifically:</p>
<ol class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-decimal flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Add <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> type to Phase 1a</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Rewrite <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">getLegalMoves</code> and <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">applyMove</code> to accept it in Phase 1b</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Add RuleSet-parameterized test cases to Phase 1c (same position, different rules, different expected outcomes)</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Add a <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RULESETS</code> presets file to the shared package</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Add a game creation UI for preset selection + custom rule builder to Phase 5</li>
</ol>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Want me to update the PRD with these changes, or write the <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> type and presets definitions as a standalone spec the agent can implement directly?</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="flex justify-start opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-within:opacity-100 transition" role="group" aria-label="Message actions"><div class="text-text-300"><div class="text-text-300 flex items-stretch justify-between"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" class="cds-reset group/btn relative isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none border-0 outline-none rounded h-control font-sans text-body font-medium [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast focus-visible:shadow-focus text-primary aria-pressed:text-accent aspect-square w-control px-0  !text-muted hover:!text-primary" aria-label="Copiar" data-testid="action-bar-copy"><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] transition-colors duration-fast group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-expanded/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover inset-0 group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent cds-btn-squish "></span><span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 "><span class="relative"><span data-cds="Icon" class="transition-all opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true" style="font-family: var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable); opacity: 1; transform: none;"><div class="contents"><div data-is-streaming="false" class="group relative relative pb-3" style="opacity: 1; transform: none;"><h2 class="sr-only select-none">Claude respondió: Yes, but the impact is uneven across phases.</h2><div class="font-claude-response relative leading-[1.65rem] [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:bg-bg-000/50 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-0.5 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-border-400 [&amp;_.ignore-pre-bg&gt;div]:bg-transparent [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Yes, but the impact is uneven across phases. Let me break it down honestly:</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Phase 1 — Shared Package</strong>
Heaviest impact. This is where the change lives. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">getLegalMoves</code> and <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">applyMove</code> go from simple functions to ruleset-driven ones. The test suite also needs to grow significantly — you're not just testing one ruleset, you're testing behavior differences across them. Expect this phase to take roughly 2-3x longer than originally scoped. The upside is everything above gets absorbed here so nothing leaks downstream.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Phase 2 — AI Microservice</strong>
Moderate impact. The search algorithm (negamax) itself doesn't change at all — it's ruleset-agnostic. What changes is the evaluation function, which needs to weight pieces differently depending on the ruleset (a flying king in international draughts is worth far more than a short king in English). You also need to pass <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> through to <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">getLegalMoves</code> calls inside the search tree. Not a rewrite, but not trivial either.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Phase 3 — MongoDB</strong>
Minimal. Just add a <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ruleset</code> field to the game document. One extra field, store it as a JSON object, done.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Phase 4 — Web API</strong>
Minimal. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> gets passed in on game creation and forwarded to the AI service on every move request. The API routes themselves don't care about the rules.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Phase 5 — Frontend</strong>
Moderate but isolated. The board component itself doesn't change — it just renders whatever state it receives. The only new work is the game creation UI: a preset picker dropdown and optionally a custom rule builder. The custom builder is the one wildcard — it can be as simple or as complex as you want it to be.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Phase 6 — Docker</strong>
Zero impact.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Phase 7 — Polish</strong>
Near zero. Maybe surface the active ruleset name somewhere in the game UI.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>The honest summary</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The change is largely absorbed by Phase 1, with moderate ripple into Phases 2 and 5. Phases 3, 4, 6, and 7 are essentially untouched. Since Phase 1 hasn't been signed off yet (the debugger still has open bugs), you're catching this at the best possible moment — the cost of doing it now is a Phase 1 rewrite. The cost of doing it after Phase 2 or later would mean unwinding the AI engine and API layer too.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Do it now.</strong> The PRD update is straightforward and the implementation delta is mostly concentrated in one place.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="flex justify-start opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-within:opacity-100 transition" role="group" aria-label="Message actions"><div class="text-text-300"><div class="text-text-300 flex items-stretch justify-between"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" class="cds-reset group/btn relative isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none border-0 outline-none rounded h-control font-sans text-body font-medium [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast focus-visible:shadow-focus text-primary aria-pressed:text-accent aspect-square w-control px-0  !text-muted hover:!text-primary" aria-label="Copiar" data-testid="action-bar-copy"><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] transition-colors duration-fast group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-expanded/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover inset-0 group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent cds-btn-squish "></span><span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 "><span class="relative"><span data-cds="Icon" class="transition-all opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true" style="font-family: var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable); opacity: 1; transform: none;"><div class="contents"><div data-is-streaming="false" class="group relative relative pb-3" style="opacity: 1; transform: none;"><h2 class="sr-only select-none">Claude respondió: A few things worth noting about how this patch plan is structured:</h2><div class="font-claude-response relative leading-[1.65rem] [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:bg-bg-000/50 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-0.5 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-border-400 [&amp;_.ignore-pre-bg&gt;div]:bg-transparent [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8"><div><div class="grid grid-rows-[auto_auto] min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 min-w-0"><div class="min-w-0 pl-2 py-1.5"><div class="flex items-center gap-2"><button class="group/status flex items-center gap-2 py-1 text-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left text-text-500 hover:text-text-300 flex-1 min-w-0" aria-expanded="false"><div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 min-w-0" style="opacity: 1;"><span class="truncate text-sm font-base">Creó un archivo, archivo leído</span><span class="inline-flex transition-transform duration-200 shrink-0 -rotate-90"><div style="width: 12px; height: 12px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" style="flex-shrink: 0;"><path d="M14.128 7.165a.502.502 0 0 1 .744.67l-4.5 5-.078.07a.5.5 0 0 1-.666-.07l-4.5-5-.06-.082a.501.501 0 0 1 .729-.656l.075.068L10 11.752z"></path></svg></div></span></div></button></div><span class="sr-only" role="status" aria-live="polite">Creó un archivo, archivo leído</span><div class="grid transition-[grid-template-rows] duration-300 ease-out" style="grid-template-rows: 0fr;"><div class="overflow-hidden min-w-0"></div></div></div></div><div class="row-start-2 col-start-1 relative grid isolate min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 relative z-[2] min-w-0"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">A few things worth noting about how this patch plan is structured:</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Section 17 — What Not To Do</strong> is the most important section for agentic development. LLMs tend to fill ambiguity with plausible-sounding ideas (default parameters, module-level state, inferring rules from board size). Explicitly prohibiting each failure mode cuts those off before they happen.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Documentation is baked in, not bolted on.</strong> Every function spec includes its JSDoc, and Section 5.4 requires inline comments naming the specific <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> field each branch responds to. This means future agents reading the code can trace any behavior directly back to a rule without reasoning through the whole file.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>The execution order in Section 16</strong> is strict on purpose — each step compiles and tests before the next starts. This prevents a situation where the agent writes all ten files in one pass and then has cascading type errors that are hard to isolate.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">board_size</code> is explicitly removed</strong> from the AI request body in Section 10. Small detail but worth flagging — if left alongside <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ruleset.boardSize</code> you'd have two sources of truth that could disagree.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-2 py-2"><div class="group/artifact-block relative flex text-left font-ui rounded-lg overflow-hidden border-0.5 transition duration-300 w-full hover:bg-bg-000/50 px-4 border-border-300/15 hover:border-border-200"><button type="button" class="absolute opacity: 1; transform: none;"><div class="contents"><div data-is-streaming="false" class="group relative relative pb-3" style="opacity: 1; transform: none;"><h2 class="sr-only select-none">Claude respondió: Honest answer: **it depends on what you accept as "A*".</h2><div class="font-claude-response relative leading-[1.65rem] [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:bg-bg-000/50 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-0.5 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-border-400 [&amp;_.ignore-pre-bg&gt;div]:bg-transparent [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8"><div><div class="grid grid-rows-[auto_auto] min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 min-w-0"><div class="min-w-0 pl-2 py-1.5"><div class="flex items-center gap-2"><button class="group/status flex items-center gap-2 py-1 text-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left text-text-500 hover:text-text-300 flex-1 min-w-0" aria-expanded="false"><div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 min-w-0" style="opacity: 1;"><span class="truncate text-sm font-base">Weighed A* feasibility against adversarial game constraints and implementation complexity</span><span class="inline-flex transition-transform duration-200 shrink-0 -rotate-90"><div style="width: 12px; height: 12px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" style="flex-shrink: 0;"><path d="M14.128 7.165a.502.502 0 0 1 .744.67l-4.5 5-.078.07a.5.5 0 0 1-.666-.07l-4.5-5-.06-.082a.501.501 0 0 1 .729-.656l.075.068L10 11.752z"></path></svg></div></span></div></button></div><span class="sr-only" role="status" aria-live="polite">Weighed A* feasibility against adversarial game constraints and implementation complexity</span><div class="grid transition-[grid-template-rows] duration-300 ease-out" style="grid-template-rows: 0fr;"><div class="overflow-hidden min-w-0"></div></div></div></div><div class="row-start-2 col-start-1 relative grid isolate min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 relative z-[2] min-w-0"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Honest answer: <em><em>it depends on what you accept as "A</em>".</em>*</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>The core problem</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">A* is a single-agent algorithm. It finds the cheapest path from a start state to a goal state, guided by a heuristic. Checkers has two agents actively working against each other, and no fixed goal state you're navigating toward. A* has no built-in concept of an opponent. So you can't just drop it in where minimax sits.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Your three realistic options for a learning project</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><em><em>Option 1 — Pure A</em>, single-agent (Simple)</em>*
Define the goal state as "opponent has zero pieces." Use the board evaluation function as the heuristic. A* searches for the shortest sequence of moves to reach that goal — but it completely ignores what the opponent does in between. It will find beautiful plans that fall apart the moment the opponent doesn't cooperate. Plays terribly. But it is genuinely A*, it is simple to implement, and watching it fail is itself a learning outcome.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><em><em>Option 2 — A</em> as move ordering inside minimax (Moderate)</em>*
This is actually how strong game engines work in practice. Before alpha-beta prunes the search tree, you sort the candidate moves using a heuristic score — best-looking moves first. This is A*'s heuristic idea applied inside minimax. Pruning becomes far more aggressive because good moves get examined first. The result plays well, uses both algorithms, and the contrast between "before ordering" and "after ordering" is a clear demonstrable improvement. Good teaching material.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><em><em>Option 3 — IDA</em> adapted for two players (Hard)</em>*
Iterative Deepening A* is a memory-efficient variant that uses a heuristic cutoff instead of a depth cutoff. Adapting it for adversarial play requires modifications that blur into minimax territory. Academically interesting, implementable, but complex enough that it risks becoming the whole project.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Recommendation for a learning project</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Go with <strong>Option 1 and Option 2 together</strong> as two selectable AI modes:</p>
<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><em><em>A</em> mode</em>* — pure single-agent, goal-directed. Plays naively, clearly demonstrates the algorithm, and its weaknesses against a minimax opponent are visible and educational.</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><strong>Minimax mode</strong> — negamax with A*-style move ordering (Option 2). Plays well, demonstrates how heuristic guidance improves search.</li>
</ul>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The comparison between the two becomes the learning artifact. You can literally watch A* get destroyed by minimax and understand exactly why.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Impact on the patch plan and PRD</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Moderate. The AI service would need a third <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">algorithm</code> field in the request body alongside <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">difficulty</code>. The engine folder gets a new <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">astar.ts</code> file alongside <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">search.ts</code>. The shared evaluation function gets reused by both. The game creation UI gets an algorithm selector. Nothing that breaks what's already designed — it slots in cleanly.</p>

opacity: 1; transform: none;"><div class="contents"><div data-is-streaming="false" class="group relative relative pb-3" style="opacity: 1; transform: none;"><h2 class="sr-only select-none">Claude respondió: Yes, API call testing is the right approach for Phase 2.</h2><div class="font-claude-response relative leading-[1.65rem] [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:bg-bg-000/50 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-0.5 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-border-400 [&amp;_.ignore-pre-bg&gt;div]:bg-transparent [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Yes, API call testing is the right approach for Phase 2. The AI service is a pure input→output black box — you send a board state, you get a move back. There's nothing stateful to mock, no database, no frontend. HTTP integration tests are cleaner and more meaningful than unit testing the engine internals in isolation because they test the whole pipeline: validation → engine → response. You still want a few unit tests on the evaluation function and move ordering, but the bulk of the test coverage should be API-level.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Here's the prompt:</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5"></div></div><div class="mt-4"><div class="grid grid-rows-[auto_auto] min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 min-w-0"><div class="min-w-0 pl-2 py-1.5"><div class="flex items-center gap-2"><button class="group/status flex items-center gap-2 py-1 text-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left text-text-500 hover:text-text-300 flex-1 min-w-0" aria-expanded="false"><div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 min-w-0" style="opacity: 1;"><span class="truncate text-sm font-base">Creó un archivo, archivo leído</span><span class="inline-flex transition-transform duration-200 shrink-0 -rotate-90"><div style="width: 12px; height: 12px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" style="flex-shrink: 0;"><path d="M14.128 7.165a.502.502 0 0 1 .744.67l-4.5 5-.078.07a.5.5 0 0 1-.666-.07l-4.5-5-.06-.082a.501.501 0 0 1 .729-.656l.075.068L10 11.752z"></path></svg></div></span></div></button></div><span class="sr-only" role="status" aria-live="polite">Creó un archivo, archivo leído</span><div class="grid transition-[grid-template-rows] duration-300 ease-out" style="grid-template-rows: 0fr;"><div class="overflow-hidden min-w-0"></div></div></div></div><div class="row-start-2 col-start-1 relative grid isolate min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 relative z-[2] min-w-0"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">A few decisions worth knowing about in this prompt:</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">app.fetch()</code> instead of a real HTTP server in tests.</strong> Bun and Hono both support calling the app handler directly without binding to a port. This means tests run instantly with no network setup, no port conflicts, and no teardown needed. Cleaner than spawning a process or relying on a running container.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><em><em>A</em> is scoped honestly.</em>* The prompt frames it as intentionally naive and educational, documents <em>why</em> it's weaker, and the tests only assert legal move validity — not quality. This prevents the agent from trying to "fix" A* into something it isn't.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>The noise determinism tests are written carefully.</strong> Hard mode gets a strict equality check across two calls. Easy mode gets a probabilistic test across 10 calls with a comment acknowledging the theoretical false-failure rate. An agent that writes this wrong (asserting exact moves on easy) would produce flaky tests.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>No transposition table</strong> is explicitly prohibited in Section 12. Without that constraint an agent will almost certainly add one as a "nice to have" — which introduces complexity and subtle bugs right when you're trying to validate the core engine.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-2 py-2"><div class="group/artifact-block relative flex text-left font-ui rounded-lg overflow-hidden border-0.5 transition duration-300 w-full opacity: 1; transform: none;"><div class="contents"><div data-is-streaming="false" class="group relative relative pb-3" style="opacity: 1; transform: none;"><h2 class="sr-only select-none">Claude respondió: Great catch.</h2><div class="font-claude-response relative leading-[1.65rem] [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:bg-bg-000/50 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-0.5 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-border-400 [&amp;_.ignore-pre-bg&gt;div]:bg-transparent [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Great catch. The answer is: <strong>through the <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">getLegalMoves</code> function from the shared package.</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Neither engine contains any movement logic of its own. When minimax or A* needs to know what moves are available at any given board state, it calls:</p>
<div role="group" aria-label="Código en typescript" tabindex="0" class="relative group/copy bg-bg-000/50 border-0.5 border-border-400 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent-100"><div class="sticky opacity-0 group-hover/copy:opacity-100 group-focus-within/copy:opacity-100 top-2 py-2 h-12 w-0 float-right"><div class="absolute right-0 h-8 px-2 items-center inline-flex z-10"><button class="inline-flex

background: transparent; font-family: var(--font-mono);"><code class="language-typescript" style="color: rgb(234, 236, 240); background: transparent; font-family: var(--font-mono); white-space: pre;"><span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(112, 184, 255);">getLegalMoves</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">(</span><span>board</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">,</span><span> ruleset</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">,</span><span> team</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">)</span></span></code></pre></div></div>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">That function, which lives in the shared package and was built in Phase 1, is where all the ruleset logic lives — mandatory captures, flying kings, backward captures, maximum capture filtering, all of it. The engines just consume whatever <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">getLegalMoves</code> returns without caring how those moves were generated.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">So the flow is:</p>
<div role="group" aria-label="Código" tabindex="0" class="relative group/copy bg-bg-000/50 border-0.5 border-border-400 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent-100"><div class="sticky opacity-0 group-hover/copy:opacity-100 group-focus-within/copy:opacity-100 top-2 py-2 h-12 w-0 float-right"><div class="absolute right-0 h-8 px-2 items-center inline-flex z-10"><button class="inline-flex
  items-center
  justify-center
  relative
  isolate
  shrink-0
  can-focus
  select-none
  disabled:pointer-events-none
  disabled:opacity-50
  disabled:shadow-none
  disabled:drop-shadow-none border-transparent
          transition
          font-base
          duration-300
          ease-[cubic-bezier(0.165,0.85,0.45,1)] h-8 w-8 rounded-md backdrop-blur-md _fill_10ocf_9 _ghost_10ocf_96" type="button" aria-label="Copiar al portapapeles" data-state="closed"><div class="relative"><div class="transition-all opacity-100 scale-100" style="width: 20px; height: 20px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;"><svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="transition-all opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true" style="flex-shrink: 0;"><path d="M12.5 3A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 14 4.5V6h1.5A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 17 7.5v8a1.5 1.5 0 0 1-1.5 1.5h-8A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 6 15.5V14H4.5A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 3 12.5v-8A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 4.5 3zm1.5 9.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 1-1.5 1.5H7v1.5a.5.5 0 0 0 .5.5h8a.5.5 0 0 0 .5-.5v-8a.5.5 0 0 0-.5-.5H14zM4.5 4a.5.5 0 0 0-.5.5v8a.5.5 0 0 0 .5.5h8a.5.5 0 0 0 .5-.5v-8a.5.5 0 0 0-.5-.5z"></path></svg></div><div class="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center"><div class="transition-all opacity-0 scale-50" style="width: 20px; height: 20px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;"><svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="transition-all opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true" style="flex-shrink: 0;"><path d="M15.188 5.11a.5.5 0 0 1 .752.626l-.056.084-7.5 9a.5.5 0 0 1-.738.033l-3.5-3.5-.064-.078a.501.501 0 0 1 .693-.693l.078.064 3.113 3.113 7.15-8.58z"></path></svg></div></div></div></button></div></div><div class="overflow-x-auto"><pre class="code-block__code !my-0 !rounded-lg !text-sm !leading-relaxed p-3.5" style="color: rgb(234, 236, 240); background: transparent; font-family: var(--font-mono);"><code style="color: rgb(234, 236, 240); background: transparent; font-family: var(--font-mono); white-space: pre-wrap;"><span><span>Engine receives board + ruleset + team
</span></span><span>       ↓
</span><span>Calls getLegalMoves(board, ruleset, team)
</span><span>       ↓
</span><span>Shared package applies all ruleset rules
</span><span>and returns only the legal moves for that ruleset
</span><span>       ↓
</span><span>Engine searches over those moves
</span><span>(minimax scores them, A* paths through them)
</span><span>       ↓
</span><span>Engine picks the best one and returns it</span></code></pre></div></div>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">This is actually the core reason the Phase 1 / patch plan work matters so much — if <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">getLegalMoves</code> is correct and ruleset-aware, both engines automatically play by the right rules for free. The engines themselves are ruleset-agnostic. They don't need to know anything about flying kings or backward captures. They just trust whatever moves the shared package hands them.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">It also means if you add a new ruleset later, you only touch <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ruleset.ts</code> and <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">board.ts</code>. The engines require zero changes.</p></div></div></div></div></div><div class="flex justify-start opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-within:opacity-100 transition" role="group" aria-label="Message actions"><div class="text-text-300"><div class="text-text-300 flex items-stretch justify-between"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" class="cds-reset group/btn relative isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none border-0 outline-none rounded h-control font-sans text-body font-medium [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast focus-visible:shadow-focus text-primary aria-pressed:text-accent aspect-square w-control px-0  !text-muted hover:!text-primary" aria-label="Copiar" data-testid="action-bar-copy"><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] transition-colors duration-fast group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-expanded/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover inset-0 group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent cds-btn-squish "></span><span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 "><span class="relative"><span data-cds="Icon" class="transition-all opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true" style="font-family: var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable); overflow: hidden; position: relative;"><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">FROM AGENT:
Phase 3: MongoDB Setup - Schema and Models
From PRD.txt:
- 
Task 3a: Write Mongoose schema and model for Game collection
- 
Task 3b: Write and test all DB access functions (create, read, update)
From GitHub Issue #3:
- 
Write Mongoose schema and model for Game collection
- 
Write and test all DB access functions (create, read, update)
- 
Fields required: board_size, mode, difficulty, ai_team, board, turn, status, move_count, history, created_at, updated_at
Data Model Details (from PRD):
The Game collection should have this structure:
{
  _id:           ObjectId
  board_size:    Number       // 8 or 10
  mode:          String       // "pvp" | "pva" | "ava"
  difficulty:    String       // "easy" | "medium" | "hard" | null (pvp)
  ai_team:       String       // "red" | "black" | null (pvp)
  board:         String       // current board string
  turn:          String       // "red" | "black"
  status:        String       // "active" | "red_wins" | "black_wins" | "draw"
  move_count:    Number
  history:       [            // array of all moves in order
    {
      from:            [Number, Number]
      to:              [Number, Number]
      captures:        [[Number, Number]]
      promotion:       Boolean
      board_after:     String
      timestamp:       Date
    }
  ]
  created_at:    Date
  updated_at:    Date
}
Implementation Plan:
1. 
Create Mongoose schema definition
2. 
Implement model with proper TypeScript types
3. 
Write DB access functions:
- 
createGame()
- 
getGameById()
- 
updateGame()
- 
addMoveToHistory()
4. 
Write unit tests for all DB operations
5. 
Ensure proper validation and error handling
The phase focuses on MongoDB integration layer that will be used by the Web API in Phase 4.</p><div class="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-12 bg-gradient-to-t from-bg-300 to-transparent pointer-events-none"></div></div><button class="pb-3 pt-1 text-xs text-text-500/80 hover:text-text-100 transition w-3/4 text-left rounded-lg">Mostrar más</button></div></div></div><div class="flex justify-start opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-within:opacity-100 transition" role="group" aria-label="Message actions"><div class="text-text-300"><div class="text-text-300 flex items-stretch justify-between"><span class="text-text-500 text-xs flex items-center mr-2">28 may</span><div class="flex items-center"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" class="cds-reset group/btn relative isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none border-0 outline-none rounded h-control font-sans text-body font-medium [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast focus-visible:shadow-focus text-primary aria-pressed:text-accent aspect-square w-control px-0  !text-muted hover:!text-primary" aria-label="Reintentar"><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] transition-colors duration-fast group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-expanded/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover inset-0 group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent cds-btn-squish "></span><span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" aria-hidden="true" style="font-family: var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable); The patch plan explicitly changed this in Section 12:</p>
<blockquote class="ml-2 border-l-4 border-border-300/10 pl-4 text-text-300">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">REMOVE: <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">board_size: Number</code>
ADD: <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ruleset: { type: mongoose.Schema.Types.Mixed, required: true }</code></p>
</blockquote>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">If you let it proceed with the plan as written, Phase 4 will be built on a schema that contradicts the patch, and you'll have a conflict between what the DB stores and what the AI service expects in its request body.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Send this correction back to the agent before it writes anything:</strong></p>
<blockquote class="ml-2 border-l-4 border-border-300/10 pl-4 text-text-300">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Before implementing Phase 3, apply the following corrections to your plan. These come from PATCH_ruleset_modularity.txt Section 12 which supersedes the original PRD data model.</p>
<ol class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-decimal flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Remove the <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">board_size</code> field entirely. Board size is now read from <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ruleset.boardSize</code>.</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Add a <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ruleset</code> field of type <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">mongoose.Schema.Types.Mixed</code>, required. This stores the complete RuleSet object as a subdocument.</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">In the history subdocument, the move entry already has <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">from</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">to</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">captures</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">promotion</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">board_after</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">timestamp</code> — keep those. No changes needed there.</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">The <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">createGame()</code> function must accept a <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ruleset</code> parameter, not a <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">boardSize</code> parameter. Validate the ruleset using <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">validateRuleSet()</code> from the shared package before saving.</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Any function that currently references <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">board_size</code> must reference <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">game.ruleset.boardSize</code> instead.</li>
</ol>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Once those corrections are applied to your plan, proceed with implementation.</p>
</blockquote>

transform: none;"><div class="flex flex-row gap-2 relative"><div class="flex-1"><div data-testid="user-message" class="font-large !font-user-message py-0.5 grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 relative [&amp;_ul]:!space-y-0 [&amp;_ol]:!space-y-0 [&amp;_ul]:pl-8 [&amp;_ol]:pl-8" style="max-height: 200px; overflow: hidden; position: relative;"><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">Updated Phase 3 Implementation Plan
Key Corrections from Exploration:
1. 
Remove board_size field - Board size is now determined by ruleset.boardSize
2. 
Add ruleset field - Complete RuleSet object stored as subdocument
3. 
Validation requirement - createGame() must accept and validate ruleset using validateRuleSet() from shared package
4. 
Field references - Any code referencing board_size must use game.ruleset.boardSize instead
Current State Analysis:
- 
Shared package already has comprehensive ruleset support with validation
- 
AI service already uses ruleset-based approach (not board_size)
- 
Phase 1 &amp; 2 implementations are ruleset-aware
Updated Data Model:
{
  _id:           ObjectId
  ruleset:       RuleSet       // Complete ruleset object (required, validated)
  mode:          String       // "pvp" | "pva" | "ava"
  difficulty:    String       // "easy" | "medium" | "hard" | null (pvp)
  ai_team:       String       // "red" | "black" | null (pvp)
  board:         String       // current board string
  turn:          String       // "red" | "black"
  status:        String       // "active" | "red_wins" | "black_wins" | "draw"
  move_count:    Number
  history:       [            // array of all moves in order
    {
      from:            [Number, Number]
      to:              [Number, Number]
      captures:        [[Number, Number]]
      promotion:       Boolean
      board_after:     String
      timestamp:       Date
    }
  ]
  created_at:    Date
  updated_at:    Date
}
Implementation Tasks:
1. 
Create Mongoose schema with ruleset field and proper validation
2. 
Implement createGame() that accepts ruleset parameter and validates it
3. 
Update all DB functions to use game.ruleset.boardSize instead of board_size
4. 
Write comprehensive tests covering ruleset validation and board size inference
The implementation will be fully aligned with the modular ruleset architecture already established in phases 1 and 2.</p><div class="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-12 bg-gradient-to-t from-bg-300 to-transparent pointer-events-none"></div></div><button class="pb-3 pt-1 text-xs text-text-500/80 hover:text-text-100 transition w-3/4 text-left rounded-lg">Mostrar más</button></div></div></div><div class="flex justify-start opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-within:opacity-100 transition" role="group" aria-label="Message actions"><div class="text-text-300"><div class="text-text-300 flex items-stretch justify-between"><span class="text-text-500 text-xs flex items-center mr-2">28 may</span><div class="flex items-center"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" class="cds-reset group/btn relative isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none border-0 outline-none rounded h-control font-sans text-body font-medium [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast focus-visible:shadow-focus text-primary aria-pressed:text-accent aspect-square w-control px-0  !text-muted hover:!text-primary" aria-label="Reintentar"><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] transition-colors duration-fast group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-expanded/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover inset-0 group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent cds-btn-squish "></span><span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" aria-hidden="true" style="font-family: var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable); Green light to proceed with one small note to pass along first:</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Move</code> type in the shared package (added by the patch plan) includes a <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ruleset</code> field. When the agent persists moves to the history array in MongoDB, it should <strong>strip the <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ruleset</code> field before saving</strong>. Storing it on every history entry would be redundant since the game document already has the ruleset at the top level, and on a long game it would bloat every document significantly.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The pattern should be:</p>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">When reading history back out and reconstructing full <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Move</code> objects in memory, the ruleset is re-attached from <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">game.ruleset</code>.</p>

font-feature-settings: &quot;liga&quot; 0; font-optical-sizing: auto; font-style: normal; font-variation-settings: normal; line-height: 1; width: 1em; height: 1em; display: flex; align-items: center; font-feature-settings: &quot;liga&quot; 0; font-optical-sizing: auto; font-style: normal; font-variation-settings: normal; line-height: 1; width: 1em; height: 1em; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; flex-shrink: 0; user-select: none; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 433.3;"></span><span data-cds="Icon" class="absolute inset-0 transition-all opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true" style="font-family: var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable); font-feature-settings: &quot;liga&quot; 0; font-optical-sizing: auto; font-style: normal; font-variation-settings: normal; line-height: 1; width: 1em; height: 1em; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; flex-shrink: 0; user-select: none; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 433.3;"></span></span></span></button><div class="flex items-center"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="[content-visibility:auto] [contain-intrinsic-size:auto_400px] pb-8 -mb-8 print:[content-visibility:visible]"><div data-test-render-count="2"><div class="group" style="height: auto; opacity: 1; transform: none;"><div class="contents"><div data-is-streaming="false" class="group relative relative pb-3" style="opacity: 1; transform: none;"><h2 class="sr-only select-none">Claude respondió: Phase 3 is a database layer, not an HTTP service, so you can't use API calls the way you did in Phase 2.</h2><div class="font-claude-response relative leading-[1.65rem] [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:bg-bg-000/50 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-0.5 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-border-400 [&amp;_.ignore-pre-bg&gt;div]:bg-transparent [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Phase 3 is a database layer, not an HTTP service, so you can't use API calls the way you did in Phase 2. You have two realistic options:</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Option 1 — Unit tests with a mocked MongoDB connection (fast, no infrastructure)</strong>
Use a library like <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">mongodb-memory-server</code> which spins up a real MongoDB instance in memory — no Docker, no external connection needed. Tests run in isolation, reset between each test, and are fully self-contained. This is the standard approach for testing Mongoose models.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The test suite connects to the in-memory DB before tests run and drops it after. Each test starts with a clean slate.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">What you test:</p>
<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">createGame()</code> saves a document with the correct shape</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">createGame()</code> rejects an invalid ruleset via <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">validateRuleSet()</code></li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">getGameById()</code> returns null for a nonexistent ID</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">getGameById()</code> returns the correct document for a known ID</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">updateGame()</code> persists changes correctly</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">addMoveToHistory()</code> appends a move and does not duplicate it</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">addMoveToHistory()</code> strips the ruleset field before saving</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">move_count</code> increments correctly on each <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">addMoveToHistory()</code> call</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">updated_at</code> changes after every update operation</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">Board string length is validated against <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ruleset.boardSize²</code></li>
</ul>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Option 2 — Integration tests against the real Docker MongoDB (slower, closer to production)</strong>
Spin up just the MongoDB service from docker compose and run tests against it. Slower and requires Docker running, but tests the actual connection string, auth, and index behavior.</p>
<div role="group" aria-label="Código en bash" tabindex="0" class="relative group/copy bg-bg-000/50 border-0.5 border-border-400 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent-100"><div class="sticky opacity-0 group-hover/copy:opacity-100 group-focus-within/copy:opacity-100 top-2 py-2 h-12 w-0 float-right"><div class="absolute right-0 h-8 px-2 items-center inline-flex z-10"><button class="inline-flex

  disabled:drop-shadow-none border-transparent

</span></span><span><span>bun </span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(251, 173, 96);">test</span><span> apps/web/tests/db/</span></span></code></pre></div></div>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>Recommendation: Option 1 as the primary suite, Option 2 as a smoke test.</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">mongodb-memory-server</code> gives you fast, reliable, CI-friendly tests. The Docker smoke test just verifies the connection string and container setup work — one test that creates and reads a game document is enough for that.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>One thing to flag to the agent:</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">mongodb-memory-server</code> needs to be added as a dev dependency. Tell the agent to add it explicitly:</p>
<div role="group" aria-label="Código en bash" tabindex="0" class="relative group/copy bg-bg-000/50 border-0.5 border-border-400 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent-100"><div class="sticky opacity-0 group-hover/copy:opacity-100 group-focus-within/copy:opacity-100 top-2 py-2 h-12 w-0 float-right"><div class="absolute right-0 h-8 px-2 items-center inline-flex z-10"><button class="inline-flex

Let me go through them by severity.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>🔴 Functional Issues</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>1. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Move</code> type mismatch in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">addMoveToHistory</code></strong>
The <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Move</code> type from the shared package has: <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">from, to, captures, promotion, ruleset</code>. It does NOT have <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">board_after</code> or <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">timestamp</code>. The test passes an object with those fields and the history schema expects them, but this will be a TypeScript error. A separate type is needed:</p>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>2. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">addMoveToHistory</code> never updates the current <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">board</code> field</strong>
After a move the game document's <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">board</code> field still holds the pre-move state. Phase 4 will read <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">game.board</code> to get the current position and get stale data. The update should also set the current board:</p>

<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">buildStandardLayout</code> is imported twice — once near the top and again on the very last line. This will cause a TypeScript error at runtime:</p>
<div role="group" aria-label="Código en typescript" tabindex="0" class="relative group/copy bg-bg-000/50 border-0.5 border-border-400 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent-100"><div class="sticky opacity-0 group-hover/copy:opacity-100 group-focus-within/copy:opacity-100 top-2 py-2 h-12 w-0 float-right"><div class="absolute right-0 h-8 px-2 items-center inline-flex z-10"><button class="inline-flex

</span><span><span></span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(129, 136, 152);">// Last line — duplicate, must be deleted</span><span>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>2. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">updated_at</code> test will fail</strong>
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">updateGame()</code> uses <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">findByIdAndUpdate()</code> which bypasses Mongoose pre-save hooks. The <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">updated_at</code> field only auto-updates via the <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">pre('save')</code> hook, which <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">findByIdAndUpdate</code> never triggers. The test asserting <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">updated_at</code> changed will either fail or be flaky. Fix by explicitly setting it in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">updateGame</code>:</p>
<div role="group" aria-label="Código en typescript" tabindex="0" class="relative group/copy bg-bg-000/50 border-0.5 border-border-400 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent-100"><div class="sticky opacity-0 group-hover/copy:opacity-100 group-focus-within/copy:opacity-100 top-2 py-2 h-12 w-0 float-right"><div class="absolute right-0 h-8 px-2 items-center inline-flex z-10"><button class="inline-flex

</span></span><span><span></span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">)</span></span></code></pre></div></div>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>3. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">@types/mongoose</code> is wrong package</strong>
Mongoose v9 ships its own TypeScript types — <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">@types/mongoose</code> is the community types package for Mongoose v5 and will conflict. Remove it everywhere it appears.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>4. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">mongoose</code> in root <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">package.json</code></strong>
Mongoose was added to the workspace root <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">dependencies</code>. It belongs only in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">apps/web/package.json</code>. Remove it from the root.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>🟡 Minor Issues</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>5. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">buildStandardLayout</code> reimplemented instead of imported</strong>
Per patch plan Section 3.4, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">buildStandardLayout</code> lives in the shared package (<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">packages/shared/src/ruleset.ts</code>). <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">game.service.ts</code> should import it from <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">@checkers/shared</code> rather than reimplementing it. Having two implementations risks them drifting out of sync.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>6. Unused imports/variables</strong></p>
<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Types</code> imported from mongoose in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">game.model.ts</code> but never used</li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">totalCells</code> declared in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">buildStandardLayout</code> in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">game.service.ts</code> but never used</li>
</ul>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>7. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">addMoveToHistory</code> makes two sequential DB calls</strong>
It calls <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">findById</code> to get the current turn, then <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">findByIdAndUpdate</code>. This is a minor race condition risk. The turn could be passed in as a parameter from the caller who already has the game state, reducing it to one atomic operation.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>8. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">PHASE3_COMPLETION_SUMMARY.md</code> still in the PR</strong>
This was flagged last review. Still here.</p>

overflow: hidden; position: relative;"><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">ok, now phase 4. The agent suggsted the following.</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">Phase 4: Web API
Phase 4 focuses on implementing the Web API for the checkers game. This phase includes:
1. 
Scaffold Hono API routes under apps/web/server/
2. 
Implement API endpoints:
- 
POST /api/game: Create a new game
- 
GET /api/game/:gameId/state: Get current game state
- 
GET /api/game/:gameId/legal: Get legal moves for a given piece
- 
POST /api/game/:gameId/move: Submit a player move
- 
POST /api/game/:gameId/resign: Resign the game
3. 
Integration with AI Microservice: When it is the AI's turn, the web service will call the AI microservice, apply the response, and save the updated state to MongoDB.
4. 
Integration tests for all API routes.
Execution Plan for Phase 4
1. 
Setup MongoDB Schema and Models
- 
Define the Mongoose schema for the games collection.
- 
Implement DB access functions (create, read, update).
2. 
Implement API Endpoints
- 
Scaffold the Hono API routes.
- 
Implement each endpoint with proper validation and logic.
3. 
Integration with AI Microservice
- 
Ensure the web service calls the AI microservice when it is the AI's turn.
- 
Apply the AI's move and save the updated state to MongoDB.
4. 
Testing
- 
Write and run integration tests for all API routes.</p><div class="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 h-12 bg-gradient-to-t from-bg-300 to-transparent pointer-events-none"></div></div><button class="pb-3 pt-1 text-xs text-text-500/80 hover:text-text-100 transition w-3/4 text-left rounded-lg">Mostrar más</button></div></div></div><div class="flex justify-start opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-within:opacity-100 transition" role="group" aria-label="Message actions"><div class="text-text-300"><div class="text-text-300 flex items-stretch justify-between"><span class="text-text-500 text-xs flex items-center mr-2">6:25 p. m.</span><div class="flex items-center"><button type="button" data-cds="Button" class="cds-reset group/btn relative isolate inline-flex shrink-0 items-center justify-center gap-1.5 whitespace-nowrap select-none border-0 outline-none rounded h-control font-sans text-body font-medium [&amp;:disabled:not([aria-busy])]:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none transition-shadow duration-fast focus-visible:shadow-focus text-primary aria-pressed:text-accent aspect-square w-control px-0  !text-muted hover:!text-primary" aria-label="Reintentar"><span aria-hidden="true" class="absolute -z-[1] rounded-[inherit] transition-colors duration-fast group-focus-visible/btn:shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_var(--cds-page-bg)] bg-transparent group-hover/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover group-aria-expanded/btn:bg-fill-ghost-hover inset-0 group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent group-hover/btn:group-aria-pressed/btn:bg-accent cds-btn-squish "></span><span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 "><span data-cds="Icon" aria-hidden="true" style="font-family: var(--font-anthropicons, Anthropicons-Variable);
