opacity: 1; transform: none;"><div class="contents"><div data-is-streaming="false" class="group relative relative pb-3" style="opacity: 1; transform: none;"><h2 class="sr-only select-none">Claude respondió: The agent's plan is correct at a high level but far too vague to execute safely.</h2><div class="font-claude-response relative leading-[1.65rem] [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:bg-bg-000/50 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-0.5 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-border-400 [&amp;_.ignore-pre-bg&gt;div]:bg-transparent [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8"><div><div class="grid grid-rows-[auto_auto] min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 min-w-0"><div class="min-w-0 pl-2 py-1.5"><div class="flex items-center gap-2"><button class="group/status flex items-center gap-2 py-1 text-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left text-text-500 hover:text-text-300 flex-1 min-w-0" aria-expanded="false"><div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 min-w-0" style="opacity: 1;"><span class="truncate text-sm font-base">Architected comprehensive Web API specification with detailed endpoint requirements</span><span class="inline-flex transition-transform duration-200 shrink-0 -rotate-90"><div style="width: 12px; height: 12px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" style="flex-shrink: 0;"><path d="M14.128 7.165a.502.502 0 0 1 .744.67l-4.5 5-.078.07a.5.5 0 0 1-.666-.07l-4.5-5-.06-.082a.501.501 0 0 1 .729-.656l.075.068L10 11.752z"></path></svg></div></span></div></button></div><span class="sr-only" role="status" aria-live="polite">Architected comprehensive Web API specification with detailed endpoint requirements</span><div class="grid transition-[grid-template-rows] duration-300 ease-out" style="grid-template-rows: 0fr;"><div class="overflow-hidden min-w-0"></div></div></div></div><div class="row-start-2 col-start-1 relative grid isolate min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 relative z-[2] min-w-0"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">The agent's plan is correct at a high level but far too vague to execute safely. It will make a dozen silent decisions — how validation works, what error shapes look like, how the AI turn loop is triggered, what the test setup looks like — and you won't know until Phase 5 breaks against it. Write the full prompt now.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="mt-4"><div class="grid grid-rows-[auto_auto] min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 min-w-0"><div class="min-w-0 pl-2 py-1.5"><div class="flex items-center gap-2"><button class="group/status flex items-center gap-2 py-1 text-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left text-text-500 hover:text-text-300 flex-1 min-w-0" aria-expanded="false"><div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 min-w-0" style="opacity: 1;"><span class="truncate text-sm font-base">Creó un archivo, archivo leído</span><span class="inline-flex transition-transform duration-200 shrink-0 -rotate-90"><div style="width: 12px; height: 12px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;"><svg width="16" height="16" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" aria-hidden="true" style="flex-shrink: 0;"><path d="M14.128 7.165a.502.502 0 0 1 .744.67l-4.5 5-.078.07a.5.5 0 0 1-.666-.07l-4.5-5-.06-.082a.501.501 0 0 1 .729-.656l.075.068L10 11.752z"></path></svg></div></span></div></button></div><span class="sr-only" role="status" aria-live="polite">Creó un archivo, archivo leído</span><div class="grid transition-[grid-template-rows] duration-300 ease-out" style="grid-template-rows: 0fr;"><div class="overflow-hidden min-w-0"></div></div></div></div><div class="row-start-2 col-start-1 relative grid isolate min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 relative z-[2] min-w-0"><div><div class="standard-markdown grid-cols-1 grid [&amp;_&gt;_*]:min-w-0 gap-3 standard-markdown"><p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">A few decisions in this prompt worth knowing about:</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>The AI vs AI loop is explicitly left to the frontend.</strong> The server handles exactly one AI move per request. The alternative — a server-side loop that fires both AI turns before responding — would cause request timeouts on hard difficulty and make the frontend unable to show intermediate states. Documented in Section 12 so the agent doesn't "helpfully" implement it.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>AI service errors don't corrupt game state.</strong> The player's move is saved first, then the AI turn runs. If the AI fails, the player move is already committed and the AI turn is pending. Section 11 requires this to be documented in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ai.client.ts</code> so Phase 5 knows how to handle that edge case on the frontend (show an error, offer a retry button).</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">algorithm</code> field was missing from the Phase 3 schema</strong> — caught and added here rather than leaving it as a silent gap for the agent to discover mid-implementation.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>The mock pattern</strong> for the AI client in tests is called out explicitly because Bun's module mocking syntax differs from Jest and agents frequently get this wrong without guidance.</p></div></div><div class="flex flex-col gap-2 py-2"><div class="group/artifact-block relative flex text-left font-ui <p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">From now on <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">git push</code> pushes to both simultaneously. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">git pull</code> and <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">git fetch</code> only touch GitHub.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">One thing to note — if either remote is down when you push, the whole push fails. opacity: 1; transform: none;"><div class="contents"><div data-is-streaming="false" class="group relative relative pb-3" style="opacity: 1; transform: none;"><h2 class="sr-only select-none">Claude respondió: There are several significant issues here.</h2><div class="font-claude-response relative leading-[1.65rem] [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:bg-bg-000/50 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-0.5 [&amp;_pre&gt;div]:border-border-400 [&amp;_.ignore-pre-bg&gt;div]:bg-transparent [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.standard-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pl-2 [&amp;_.progressive-markdown_:is(p,blockquote,ul,ol,h1,h2,h3,h4,h5,h6)]:pr-8"><div><div class="grid grid-rows-[auto_auto] min-w-0"><div class="row-start-1 col-start-1 min-w-0"><div class="min-w-0 pl-2 py-1.5"><div class="flex items-center gap-2"><button class="group/status flex items-center gap-2 py-1 text-sm transition-colors cursor-pointer text-left text-text-500 hover:text-text-300 flex-1 min-w-0" aria-expanded="false"><div class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 min-w-0" style="opacity: 1;"><span class="truncate text-sm font-base">Identified twenty critical implementation gaps across routes, schemas, and configuration</span><span class="inline-flex transition-transform duration-200 shrink-0 -rotate-90"><div style="width: 12px; Let me go through them by severity.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>🔴 Critical — Will Break Phase 5</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>1. Route prefix is wrong throughout</strong>
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">app.ts</code> mounts routes at <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/games</code> but the PRD specifies <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game</code>. Tests also use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/games/</code>. The frontend (Phase 5) will be built against <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game</code>. Fix <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">app.ts</code>:</p>
<div role="group" aria-label="Código en typescript" tabindex="0" class="relative group/copy bg-bg-000/50 border-0.5 border-border-400 rounded-lg focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-accent-100"><div class="sticky opacity-0 group-hover/copy:opacity-100 group-focus-within/copy:opacity-100 top-2 py-2 h-12 w-0 float-right"><div class="absolute right-0 h-8 px-2 items-center inline-flex z-10"><button class="inline-flex

  disabled:drop-shadow-none border-transparent

height: 20px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;"><svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="transition-all opacity-100 scale-100" aria-hidden="true" style="flex-shrink: 0;"><path d="M12.5 3A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 14 4.5V6h1.5A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 17 7.5v8a1.5 1.5 0 0 1-1.5 1.5h-8A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 6 15.5V14H4.5A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 3 12.5v-8A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 4.5 3zm1.5 9.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 1-1.5 1.5H7v1.5a.5.5 0 0 0 .5.5h8a.5.5 0 0 0 .5-.5v-8a.5.5 0 0 0-.5-.5H14zM4.5 4a.5.5 0 0 0-.5.5v8a.5.5 0 0 0 .5.5h8a.5.5 0 0 0 .5-.5v-8a.5.5 0 0 0-.5-.5z"></path></svg></div><div class="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center"><div class="transition-all opacity-0 scale-50" style="width: 20px; height: 20px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;"><svg width="20" height="20" viewBox="0 0 20 20" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="transition-all opacity-0 scale-50" aria-hidden="true" style="flex-shrink: 0;"><path d="M15.188 5.11a.5.5 0 0 1 .752.626l-.056.084-7.5 9a.5.5 0 0 1-.738.033l-3.5-3.5-.064-.078a.501.501 0 0 1 .693-.693l.078.064 3.113 3.113 7.15-8.58z"></path></svg></div></div></div></button></div></div><div class="text-text-500 font-small p-3.5 pb-0">typescript</div><div class="overflow-x-auto"><pre class="code-block__code !my-0 !rounded-lg !text-sm !leading-relaxed p-3.5" style="color: rgb(234, 236, 240); background: transparent; font-family: var(--font-mono);"><code class="language-typescript" style="color: rgb(234, 236, 240); background: transparent; font-family: var(--font-mono); white-space: pre;"><span><span>app</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">.</span><span class="token token property-access" style="color: rgb(112, 184, 255);">route</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">(</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(155, 233, 99);">'/api/game'</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">,</span><span> gameRoutes</span><span class="token token" style="color: rgb(211, 215, 222);">)</span></span></code></pre></div></div>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]">Then update all test paths accordingly.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>2. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">algorithm</code> enum is wrong in model and service</strong>
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">game.model.ts</code> and <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">game.service.ts</code> both use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">['minimax', 'alphabeta', 'mcts', 'random']</code>. The only valid values per the entire project spec are <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">'minimax'</code> and <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">'astar'</code>. This will silently reject valid requests and accept invalid ones.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>3. AI turn never triggers in pva mode</strong>
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">POST /:gameId/move</code> applies the player's move but never calls <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">triggerAiTurn</code>. The AI integration specified in the prompt is completely absent from the route handler. After a player move in pva mode, the AI must move before the response is sent.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>4. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">triggerAiTurn</code> doesn't save to DB</strong>
Per the prompt spec, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">triggerAiTurn</code> is responsible for calling <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">addMoveToHistory</code>, checking <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">isGameOver</code>, and updating game status. The current implementation just calls <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">requestAiMove</code> and returns the move data — nothing is persisted. The route handler would need to do all the saving manually, but it doesn't.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>5. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">AiServiceError</code> class is missing</strong>
The prompt required a custom error class so route handlers can catch AI failures specifically and return 503. Without it there's no way to distinguish an AI service failure from any other error, so AI failures either get swallowed or return 500.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>🔴 Critical — Wrong HTTP Status Codes</strong>
The prompt specified exact codes. These are wrong:</p>
<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">"Game is already over" → returns <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">400</code>, should be <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">409</code></li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">"Illegal move" → returns <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">400</code>, should be <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">422</code></li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2">AI service failure → no handler at all, should be <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">503</code></li>
</ul>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>🟡 Spec Violations</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>6. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">GET /:gameId/legal</code> ignores <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">row</code> and <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">col</code> params</strong>
It returns all legal moves for the whole team. The spec requires filtering by a specific piece at <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">[row, col]</code> passed as query params, with a 400 if they're missing or invalid.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>7. Response shapes don't match spec</strong></p>
<ul class="[li_&amp;]:mb-0 [li_&amp;]:mt-1 [li_&amp;]:gap-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ul]:pb-1 [&amp;:not(:last-child)_ol]:pb-1 list-disc flex flex-col gap-1 pl-8 mb-3">
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">POST /</code> returns <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">{ gameId, status, ruleset, mode, turn }</code> — spec requires <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">{ gameId, state: GameStateResponse }</code></li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">POST /:gameId/move</code> returns <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">{ status, board, turn, move_count }</code> — spec requires <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">{ move: MoveResponse, state: GameStateResponse }</code></li>
<li class="font-claude-response-body whitespace-normal break-words pl-2"><code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">GET /:gameId/state</code> is missing <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">gameId</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">difficulty</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ai_team</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">algorithm</code> fields</li>
</ul>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>8. Resign uses <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">player</code> field instead of <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">team</code></strong>
The prompt spec, the model, and everything else uses <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">team</code>. The route reads <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">body.player</code>. Tests also use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">player</code> so they pass, but Phase 5 will send <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">team</code> and it will silently break.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>9. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">index.ts</code> uses <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">@hono/node-server</code> instead of <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Bun.serve()</code></strong>
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">CLAUDE.md</code> in the repo explicitly says not to use this. The prompt specified <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Bun.serve({ fetch: app.fetch, port: PORT })</code>. Also <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">@hono/node-server</code> should not be in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">package.json</code>.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>10. MongoDB connection errors are swallowed in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">index.ts</code></strong>
The prompt says "If mongoose.connect() throws, let the process exit." Instead the server logs a warning and continues running without a DB, which causes confusing downstream errors.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>11. CORS middleware ignores <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ALLOWED_ORIGIN</code> env var</strong>
Origins are hardcoded to <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">localhost:3000</code> and <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">localhost:5173</code> instead of reading from <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">process.env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN</code>.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>🟡 Test Issues</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>12. No AI service mocking in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">game.api.test.ts</code></strong>
Tests that create pva/ava games and trigger moves don't mock <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">requestAiMove</code>. In the test environment port 4000 isn't running, so these tests would hang or fail for the wrong reason.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>13. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ai.client.test.ts</code> uses wrong <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> fields</strong>
The mock ruleset uses <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">kingsCanMoveBackwards</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">maxConsecutiveJumps</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">forceCapture</code> — none of these exist in the actual <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> type. Fields should be <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">normalMoveDirections</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">kingMoveDistance</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">captureRequired</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">captureMaximum</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">captureBackward</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">promotionEndsJump</code>.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>14. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">buildStandardLayout</code> import is still duplicated at the bottom of the file. This was flagged in the Phase 3 review.</p>

import { Hono } from 'hono'
import { z } from 'zod'
import { createGame, getGameById, addMoveToHistory, updateGame } from '../../models/game.service'
import { triggerAiTurn, AiServiceError } from '../../services/ai.client'
import { type RuleSet, type Move, type GameStatus, PRESET_RULESETS } from '@checkers/shared'
import { getLegalMoves, applyMove, isGameOver, parseBoardString, serializeBoardString } from '@checkers/shared'
// Response types
type GameStateResponse = {
  gameId: string
  board: string
  ruleset: RuleSet
  turn: 'red' | 'black'
  status: GameStatus
  mode: 'pvp' | 'pva' | 'ava'
  difficulty?: 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard'
  ai_team?: 'red' | 'black'
  algorithm?: 'minimax' | 'astar'
  move_count: number
}
type MoveResponse = {
  from: [number, number]
  to: [number, number]
  captures: [number, number][]
  promotion: boolean
}
// Helper to build game state response
function buildGameStateResponse(game: {
  _id: string | { toString(): string }
  board: string
  ruleset: RuleSet
  turn: 'red' | 'black'
  status: GameStatus
  mode: 'pvp' | 'pva' | 'ava'
  difficulty?: 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard'
  ai_team?: 'red' | 'black'
  algorithm?: 'minimax' | 'astar'
  move_count: number
}): GameStateResponse {
  return {
    gameId: typeof game._id === 'string' ? game._id : game._id.toString(),
    board: game.board,
    ruleset: game.ruleset,
    turn: game.turn,
    status: game.status,
    mode: game.mode,
    difficulty: game.difficulty,
    ai_team: game.ai_team,
    algorithm: game.algorithm,
    move_count: game.move_count
  }
}
// Create game router
const gameRouter = new Hono()
// Zod schemas for validation
const createGameSchema = z.object({
  ruleset: z.union([
    z.object({ preset: z.enum(['english', 'international', 'brazilian', 'russian', 'pool']) }),
    z.custom&lt;RuleSet&gt;()
  ]),
  mode: z.enum(['pvp', 'pva', 'ava']),
  difficulty: z.enum(['easy', 'medium', 'hard']).optional(),
  ai_team: z.enum(['red', 'black']).optional(),
  algorithm: z.enum(['minimax', 'astar']).optional()
})
const moveSchema = z.object({
  from: z.tuple([z.number(), z.number()]),
  to: z.tuple([z.number(), z.number()]),
  captures: z.array(z.tuple([z.number(), z.number()])),
  promotion: z.boolean()
})
const positionQuerySchema = z.object({
  row: z.coerce.number().int(),
  col: z.coerce.number().int()
})
// POST / - Create a new game
gameRouter.post('/', async (c) =&gt; {
  try {
    const body = await c.req.json()
    const validation = createGameSchema.safeParse(body)</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    if (!validation.success) {
      return c.json({ error: 'Invalid request body', details: validation.error.errors }, 400)
    }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    const { ruleset, mode, difficulty, ai_team, algorithm } = validation.data
    // Convert preset to full ruleset if needed
    let fullRuleset: RuleSet
    if ('preset' in ruleset) {
      fullRuleset = PRESET_RULESETS[ruleset.preset]
    } else {
      fullRuleset = ruleset
    }
    const game = await createGame(fullRuleset, mode, difficulty, ai_team, algorithm)
    return c.json({
      gameId: game._id,
      state: buildGameStateResponse(game)
    }, 201)
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      return c.json({ error: error.message }, 400)
    }
    return c.json({ error: 'Internal server error' }, 500)
  }
})

    if (!gameId || gameId.length !== 24) {

    }
    return c.json({ error: 'Internal server error' }, 500)
  }

// GET /:gameId/legal - Get all legal moves for current player
gameRouter.get('/:gameId/legal', async (c) =&gt; {
  try {
    const gameId = c.req.param('gameId')
    // Validate gameId format
    if (!gameId || gameId.length !== 24) {
      return c.json({ error: 'Invalid game ID format' }, 400)
    }
    const game = await getGameById(gameId)
    if (!game) {
      return c.json({ error: 'Game not found' }, 404)
    }
    const board = parseBoardString(game.board, game.ruleset)
    const allLegalMoves = getLegalMoves(board, game.ruleset, game.turn)
    // Validate and extract query parameters
    const queryParams = c.req.query()
    const queryValidation = positionQuerySchema.safeParse(queryParams)
    if (!queryValidation.success) {
      return c.json({ error: 'Invalid query parameters: row and col are required integers' }, 400)
    }
    const { row, col } = queryValidation.data
    // Validate the cell belongs to the current player
    if (row &lt; 0 || row &gt;= board.length || col &lt; 0 || col &gt;= board[0]?.length) {
      return c.json({ error: 'Position out of bounds' }, 400)
    }
    const cell = board[row][col]
    if (!cell.piece) {
      return c.json({ error: 'No piece at specified position' }, 400)
    }
    if (cell.piece.team !== game.turn) {
      return c.json({ error: 'It is not your turn to move this piece' }, 400)
    }
    // Filter legal moves to only those from the specified position
    const legalMoves = allLegalMoves.filter(m =&gt; m.from[0] === row &amp;&amp; m.from[1] === col)
    return c.json({
      turn: game.turn,
      legal_moves: legalMoves
    })
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error &amp;&amp; error.message.includes('Cast to ObjectId failed')) {
      return c.json({ error: 'Invalid game ID format' }, 400)
    }
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      return c.json({ error: error.message }, 400)
    }
    return c.json({ error: 'Internal server error' }, 500)
  }
})
// POST /:gameId/move - Make a move
gameRouter.post('/:gameId/move', async (c) =&gt; {
  try {
    const gameId = c.req.param('gameId')
    const game = await getGameById(gameId)</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    if (!game) {
      return c.json({ error: 'Game not found' }, 404)
    }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    if (game.status !== 'active') {
      return c.json({ error: 'Game is already over' }, 409)
    }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    const body = await c.req.json()
    const validation = moveSchema.safeParse(body)</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    if (!validation.success) {
      return c.json({ error: 'Invalid move format', details: validation.error.errors }, 400)
    }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    const move = validation.data</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    // Validate it's the correct player's turn
    const board = parseBoardString(game.board, game.ruleset)
    const fromCell = board[move.from[0]][move.from[1]]</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    if (!fromCell.piece || fromCell.piece.team !== game.turn) {
      return c.json({ error: 'It is not your turn to move this piece' }, 400)
    }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    // Validate the move is legal
    const legalMoves = getLegalMoves(board, game.ruleset, game.turn)
    const isLegalMove = legalMoves.some(m =&gt; 
      m.from[0] === move.from[0] &amp;&amp; 
      m.from[1] === move.from[1] &amp;&amp;
      m.to[0] === move.to[0] &amp;&amp; 
      m.to[1] === move.to[1]
    )</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    if (!isLegalMove) {
      return c.json({ error: 'Illegal move' }, 422)
    }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    // Apply the move
    const moveWithRuleset: Move = {
      ...move,
      ruleset: game.ruleset
    }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    const newBoard = applyMove(board, moveWithRuleset, game.ruleset)
    const boardAfter = serializeBoardString(newBoard, game.ruleset)</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    // Save player move to history
    const updatedGame = await addMoveToHistory(gameId, moveWithRuleset, boardAfter)</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    if (!updatedGame) {
      return c.json({ error: 'Failed to update game' }, 500)
    }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    // Check if game is over after player move
    const gameOverResult = isGameOver(newBoard, game.ruleset)
    if (gameOverResult) {
      const finalGame = await updateGame(gameId, {
        status: gameOverResult.winner === 'draw' ? 'draw' : <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">${gameOverResult.winner}_wins</code>
      })
      const moveResponse: MoveResponse = {
        from: move.from,
        to: move.to,
        captures: move.captures as [number, number][],
        promotion: move.promotion
      }
      return c.json({
        move: moveResponse,
        state: finalGame ? buildGameStateResponse(finalGame) : buildGameStateResponse(updatedGame)
      })
    }
    // Check if mode is pva and it's now AI's turn
    const nextTurn = updatedGame.turn
    if (game.mode === 'pva' &amp;&amp; game.ai_team === nextTurn) {
      try {
        const aiResult = await triggerAiTurn(
          gameId,
          nextTurn,
          game.difficulty as 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard',
          game.algorithm as 'minimax' | 'astar',
          boardAfter,
          game.ruleset
        )
        const playerMoveResponse: MoveResponse = {
          from: move.from,
          to: move.to,
          captures: move.captures as [number, number][],
          promotion: move.promotion
        }
        // Get updated game state after AI move
        const gameAfterAi = await getGameById(gameId)
        return c.json({
          move: playerMoveResponse,
          state: gameAfterAi ? buildGameStateResponse(gameAfterAi) : buildGameStateResponse(updatedGame)
        })
      } catch (error) {
        if (error instanceof AiServiceError) {
          return c.json({ error: 'AI service unavailable', details: error.message }, 503)
        }
        throw error
      }
    }
    const playerMoveResponse: MoveResponse = {
      from: move.from,
      to: move.to,
      captures: move.captures as [number, number][],
      promotion: move.promotion
    }
    return c.json({
      move: playerMoveResponse,
      state: buildGameStateResponse(updatedGame)
    })
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      return c.json({ error: error.message }, 400)
    }
    return c.json({ error: 'Internal server error' }, 500)
  }
})
// POST /:gameId/resign - Resign from the game

import { Context, Next } from 'hono'
// CORS middleware configuration
const allowedOrigins = process.env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN || '*'
const corsMiddleware = async (c: Context, next: Next) =&gt; {
  const origin = c.req.header('Origin')</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  const isAllowed = allowedOrigins === '*' || origin === allowedOrigins</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  if (origin &amp;&amp; isAllowed) {
    c.header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', origin)
    c.header('Access-Control-Allow-Methods', 'GET, POST, PUT, DELETE, OPTIONS')
    c.header('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', 'Content-Type, Authorization')
    c.header('Access-Control-Allow-Credentials', 'true')
  }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  // Handle preflight requests
  if (c.req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    return c.newResponse(null, 204)
  }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  await next()
}
// Request logging middleware
const loggingMiddleware = async (c: Context, next: Next) =&gt; {
  const start = Date.now()</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  await next()</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  const duration = Date.now() - start
  console.log(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">[${c.req.method}] ${c.req.path} - ${c.res.status} (${duration}ms)</code>)
}
// Error handling middleware
const errorHandlingMiddleware = async (c: Context, next: Next) =&gt; {
  try {
    await next()
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error:', err)</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    if (err instanceof Error) {
      return c.json(
        { error: err.message || 'Internal Server Error' },
        err.name === 'ValidationError' ? 400 : 500
      )
    }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    return c.json({ error: 'Internal Server Error' }, 500)
  }
}
export { corsMiddleware, loggingMiddleware, errorHandlingMiddleware }

import { Hono } from 'hono'
import { corsMiddleware, loggingMiddleware, errorHandlingMiddleware } from './middleware'
import gameRouter from './routes/game'
// Create the Hono app
const app = new Hono()
// Middleware
app.use('*', corsMiddleware)
app.use('*', loggingMiddleware)
app.use('*', errorHandlingMiddleware)
// Health check endpoint
app.get('/', (c) =&gt; {
  return c.json({ status: 'ok', message: 'Checkers API is running' })
})
// Game routes
app.route('/api/game', gameRouter)
// Not found handler
app.notFound((c) =&gt; {
  return c.json({ error: 'Not Found' }, 404)
})
export { app as default, app }

import { test, expect, describe, mock } from "bun:test";
import { requestAiMove, AiServiceError } from './ai.client';
import { type RuleSet } from '@checkers/shared';
const mockRuleset: RuleSet = {
  name: 'standard',
  boardSize: 8,
  startingLayout: 'standard',
  kingsCanMoveBackwards: true,
  maxConsecutiveJumps: 4,
  forceCapture: true,
  forceCaptureMax: true
};
const mockBoard = '-r-r-r-r#r-r-r-r#-r-r-r-r#--------#--------#b-b-b-b#-b-b-b-b#b-b-b-b-';
describe('AI Client', () =&gt; {
  describe('requestAiMove', () =&gt; {
    test('should validate inputs', async () =&gt; {
      // Test invalid team
      await expect(() =&gt;
        requestAiMove('invalid' as any, 'easy', 'minimax', mockBoard, mockRuleset)
      ).toThrow('Invalid team: must be "red" or "black"');
      // Test invalid difficulty
      await expect(() =&gt;
        requestAiMove('red', 'invalid' as any, 'minimax', mockBoard, mockRuleset)
      ).toThrow('Invalid difficulty: must be "easy", "medium", or "hard"');
      // Test invalid algorithm
      await expect(() =&gt;
        requestAiMove('red', 'easy', 'invalid' as any, mockBoard, mockRuleset)
      ).toThrow('Invalid algorithm: must be "minimax" or "astar"');
      // Test invalid board
      await expect(() =&gt;
        requestAiMove('red', 'easy', 'minimax', '' as any, mockRuleset)
      ).toThrow('Invalid board: must be a non-empty string');
    });
    test('should handle fetch errors', async () =&gt; {
      // Mock fetch to reject
      const originalFetch = global.fetch;
      global.fetch = mock(() =&gt; Promise.reject(new Error('Network error')));
      await expect(
        requestAiMove('red', 'easy', 'minimax', mockBoard, mockRuleset)
      ).rejects.toThrow('Failed to request AI move: Network error');
      global.fetch = originalFetch;
    });
    test('should handle non-ok responses', async () =&gt; {
      // Mock fetch to return non-ok response
      const originalFetch = global.fetch;
      global.fetch = mock(() =&gt; 
        Promise.resolve(new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: 'Service unavailable' }), { status: 503 }))
      );
      await expect(
        requestAiMove('red', 'easy', 'minimax', mockBoard, mockRuleset)
      ).rejects.toThrow('Service unavailable');
      global.fetch = originalFetch;
    });
    test('should handle invalid response structure', async () =&gt; {
      // Mock fetch to return invalid response
      const originalFetch = global.fetch;
      global.fetch = mock(() =&gt; 
        Promise.resolve(new Response(JSON.stringify({ invalid: 'response' }), { status: 200 }))
      );
      await expect(
        requestAiMove('red', 'easy', 'minimax', mockBoard, mockRuleset)
      ).rejects.toThrow('Invalid move coordinates in AI response');
      global.fetch = originalFetch;
    });
    test('should return valid move on success', async () =&gt; {
      // Mock fetch to return valid response
      const mockMove = {
        from: [2, 1],
        to: [3, 0],
        captures: [],
        promotion: false,
        resulting_board: mockBoard,
        algorithm: 'minimax'
      };
      const originalFetch = global.fetch;
      global.fetch = mock(() =&gt; 
        Promise.resolve(new Response(JSON.stringify(mockMove), { status: 200 }))
      );
      const result = await requestAiMove('red', 'easy', 'minimax', mockBoard, mockRuleset);
      expect(result.move).toEqual({
        from: [2, 1],
        to: [3, 0],
        captures: [],
        promotion: false
      });
      expect(result.resultingBoard).toBe(mockBoard);
      expect(result.algorithm).toBe('minimax');
      global.fetch = originalFetch;
    });
  });
  describe('AiServiceError', () =&gt; {
    test('should be an instance of Error', () =&gt; {
      const error = new AiServiceError('test error');
      expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(Error);
      expect(error.name).toBe('AiServiceError');
      expect(error.message).toBe('test error');
    });
  });
  // Note: triggerAiTurn tests removed due to database dependency
  // These should be tested via integration tests
});

ARCHIVO: checkers/apps/web/src/services/ai.client.ts
========================================
import { type Move, type RuleSet, parseBoardString, isGameOver } from '@checkers/shared';
import { addMoveToHistory, updateGame } from '../models/game.service.ts';
// Configuration for AI service
// In a real application, this would come from environment variables
const AI_SERVICE_BASE_URL = '<a href="http://localhost:4000" target="_blank" class="underline">http://localhost:4000</a>';
export class AiServiceError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'AiServiceError';
  }
}
/**
 * Requests an AI move from the AI service
 * @param team - The team the AI is playing as ('red' or 'black')
 * @param difficulty - The difficulty level ('easy', 'medium', or 'hard')
 * @param algorithm - The algorithm to use ('minimax' or 'astar')
 * @param board - The current board state as a string
 * @param ruleset - The ruleset being used
 * @returns Promise resolving to the AI's move and resulting board
 * @throws Error if the request fails or is invalid
 */
export async function requestAiMove(
  team: 'red' | 'black',
  difficulty: 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard',
  algorithm: 'minimax' | 'astar',
  board: string,
  ruleset: RuleSet
): Promise&lt;{
  move: Omit&lt;Move, 'ruleset'&gt;;
  resultingBoard: string;
  algorithm: string;
}&gt; {
  // Validate inputs
  if (!['red', 'black'].includes(team)) {
    throw new AiServiceError('Invalid team: must be "red" or "black"');
  }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  if (!['easy', 'medium', 'hard'].includes(difficulty)) {
    throw new AiServiceError('Invalid difficulty: must be "easy", "medium", or "hard"');
  }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  if (!['minimax', 'astar'].includes(algorithm)) {
    throw new AiServiceError('Invalid algorithm: must be "minimax" or "astar"');
  }</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  if (typeof board !== 'string' || board.length === 0) {
    throw new AiServiceError('Invalid board: must be a non-empty string');
  }
  try {
    const response = await fetch(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">${AI_SERVICE_BASE_URL}/move</code>, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        team,
        difficulty,
        algorithm,
        board,
        ruleset,
      }),
    });
    if (!response.ok) {
      const errorData = await response.json().catch(() =&gt; ({}));
      const errorMessage = errorData.error || <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">AI service returned status ${response.status}</code>;
      throw new AiServiceError(errorMessage);
    }
    const data = await response.json();
    // Validate response structure
    if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') {
      throw new AiServiceError('Invalid response from AI service');
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(data.from) || data.from.length !== 2 ||
        !Array.isArray(data.to) || data.to.length !== 2) {
      throw new AiServiceError('Invalid move coordinates in AI response');
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(data.captures)) {
      throw new AiServiceError('Invalid captures in AI response');
    }
    if (typeof data.promotion !== 'boolean') {
      throw new AiServiceError('Invalid promotion flag in AI response');
    }
    if (typeof data.resulting_board !== 'string') {
      throw new AiServiceError('Invalid resulting board in AI response');
    }
    if (typeof data.algorithm !== 'string') {
      throw new AiServiceError('Invalid algorithm in AI response');
    }
    return {
      move: {
        from: data.from,
        to: data.to,
        captures: data.captures,
        promotion: data.promotion,
      },
      resultingBoard: data.resulting_board,
      algorithm: data.algorithm,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof AiServiceError) {
      throw error;
    }
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      throw new AiServiceError(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Failed to request AI move: ${error.message}</code>);
    }
    throw new AiServiceError('Failed to request AI move: unknown error');
  }
}
/**
 * Triggers the AI's turn in a game
 * @param gameId - The ID of the game
 * @param team - The team the AI is playing as
 * @param difficulty - The difficulty level
 * @param algorithm - The algorithm to use
 * @param currentBoard - The current board state
 * @param ruleset - The ruleset being used
 * @returns Promise resolving to the updated game state
 * @throws AiServiceError if the AI move cannot be processed
 */
export async function triggerAiTurn(
  gameId: string,
  team: 'red' | 'black',
  difficulty: 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard',
  algorithm: 'minimax' | 'astar',
  currentBoard: string,
  ruleset: RuleSet
): Promise&lt;{
  move: Omit&lt;Move, 'ruleset'&gt;;
  resultingBoard: string;
  nextTurn: 'red' | 'black';
}&gt; {
  try {
    // Request AI move
    const { move, resultingBoard } = await requestAiMove(
      team,
      difficulty,
      algorithm,
      currentBoard,
      ruleset
    );
    // Add move to history
    const updatedGame = await addMoveToHistory(gameId, { ...move, ruleset }, resultingBoard);
    if (!updatedGame) {
      throw new AiServiceError('Failed to add AI move to history');
    }
    // Determine next turn
    const nextTurn = team === 'red' ? 'black' : 'red';
    // Check if game is over
    const board = parseBoardString(resultingBoard, ruleset);
    const gameOverResult = isGameOver(board, ruleset);
    if (gameOverResult) {
      await updateGame(gameId, {
        status: gameOverResult.winner === 'draw' ? 'draw' : <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">${gameOverResult.winner}_wins</code>
      });
    }
    return {
      move,
      resultingBoard,
      nextTurn,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof AiServiceError) {
      throw error;
    }
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      throw new AiServiceError(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Failed to trigger AI turn: ${error.message}</code>);
    }
    throw new AiServiceError('Failed to trigger AI turn: unknown error');
  }
}

ARCHIVO: checkers/apps/web/src/models/game.model.ts
========================================
import mongoose, { Schema, Document } from 'mongoose';
import { validateRuleSet, type RuleSet } from '@checkers/shared';
// Define the Move schema for history entries
const MoveSchema = new Schema({
  from: { type: [Number], required: true },
  to: { type: [Number], required: true },
  captures: { type: [[Number]], required: true },
  promotion: { type: Boolean, required: true },
  board_after: { type: String, required: true },
  timestamp: { type: Date, required: true }
}, { _id: false });
// Define the Game schema
const GameSchema = new Schema({
  ruleset: {
    type: Schema.Types.Mixed,
    required: true,
    validate: {
      validator: function(ruleset: RuleSet) {
        const errors = validateRuleSet(ruleset);
        return errors.length === 0;
      },
      message: 'Invalid ruleset configuration'
    }
  },
  mode: {
    type: String,
    enum: ['pvp', 'pva', 'ava'],
    required: true
  },
  difficulty: {
    type: String,
    enum: ['easy', 'medium', 'hard'],
    required: function(this: { mode: string }) {
      return this.mode !== 'pvp';
    }
  },
  ai_team: {
    type: String,
    enum: ['red', 'black'],
    required: function(this: { mode: string }) {
      return this.mode !== 'pvp';
    }
  },
  algorithm: {
    type: String,
    enum: ['minimax', 'astar'],
    required: function(this: { mode: string }) {
      return this.mode !== 'pvp';
    }
  },
  board: {
    type: String,
    required: true
  },
  turn: {
    type: String,
    enum: ['red', 'black'],
    required: true
  },
  status: {
    type: String,
    enum: ['active', 'red_wins', 'black_wins', 'draw'],
    required: true,
    default: 'active'
  },
  move_count: {
    type: Number,
    required: true,
    default: 0
  },
  history: {
    type: [MoveSchema],
    required: true,
    default: []
  },
  created_at: {
    type: Date,
    required: true,
    default: Date.now
  },
  updated_at: {
    type: Date,
    required: true,
    default: Date.now
  }
});
// Update the updated_at field before saving
GameSchema.pre('save', function(next) {
  this.updated_at = new Date();
  if (typeof next === 'function') {
    next();
  }
});
// Create the Game model
export const Game = mongoose.model&lt;IGame&gt;('Game', GameSchema);
// TypeScript interface for Game document
export interface IGame extends Document {
  ruleset: RuleSet;
  mode: 'pvp' | 'pva' | 'ava';
  difficulty?: 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard';
  ai_team?: 'red' | 'black';
  algorithm?: 'minimax' | 'astar';
  board: string;
  turn: 'red' | 'black';
  status: 'active' | 'red_wins' | 'black_wins' | 'draw';
  move_count: number;
  history: Array&lt;{
    from: [number, number];
    to: [number, number];
    captures: [number, number][];
    promotion: boolean;
    board_after: string;
    timestamp: Date;
  }&gt;;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

ARCHIVO: checkers/apps/web/src/models/game.service.ts
========================================
import { Game, IGame } from './game.model';
import { validateRuleSet, type RuleSet, type Move, buildStandardLayout } from '@checkers/shared';
// buildStandardLayout is imported from @checkers/shared
// Create a new game
export async function createGame(
  ruleset: RuleSet,
  mode: 'pvp' | 'pva' | 'ava',
  difficulty?: 'easy' | 'medium' | 'hard',
  ai_team?: 'red' | 'black',
  algorithm?: 'minimax' | 'astar'
): Promise&lt;IGame&gt; {
  // Validate ruleset
  const rulesetErrors = validateRuleSet(ruleset);
  if (rulesetErrors.length &gt; 0) {
    throw new Error(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Invalid ruleset: ${rulesetErrors[0]}</code>);
  }
   // Validate mode-specific fields
  if (mode !== 'pvp' &amp;&amp; (!difficulty || !ai_team || !algorithm)) {
    throw new Error('AI games require difficulty, ai_team, and algorithm');
  }
  // Create initial board using ruleset
  const initialBoard = ruleset.startingLayout === 'standard'
    ? buildStandardLayout(ruleset)
    : buildCustomLayout(ruleset);
   const game = new Game({
    ruleset,
    mode,
    difficulty: mode !== 'pvp' ? difficulty : undefined,
    ai_team: mode !== 'pvp' ? ai_team : undefined,
    algorithm: mode !== 'pvp' ? algorithm : undefined,
    board: initialBoard,
    turn: 'red', // Red always moves first
    status: 'active',
    move_count: 0,
    history: []
  });
  return await game.save();
}
// Get game by ID
export async function getGameById(gameId: string): Promise&lt;IGame | null&gt; {
  return await Game.findById(gameId);
}
// Update game state
export async function updateGame(
  gameId: string,
  updates: Partial&lt;IGame&gt;
): Promise&lt;IGame | null&gt; {
  return await Game.findByIdAndUpdate(
    gameId,
    { ...updates, updated_at: new Date() },
    { new: true }
  );
}
// Add move to game history
export async function addMoveToHistory(
  gameId: string,
  move: Move,
  boardAfter: string
): Promise&lt;IGame | null&gt; {
  // Convert move to history entry and strip ruleset
  const { ruleset: _, ...moveData } = move;
  const historyEntry = {
    ...moveData,
    board_after: boardAfter,
    timestamp: new Date()
  };</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  // Determine next turn (flip current turn)
  const game = await Game.findById(gameId);
  if (!game) return null;</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  const nextTurn = game.turn === 'red' ? 'black' : 'red';</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  return await Game.findByIdAndUpdate(
    gameId,
    {
      $push: { history: historyEntry },
      $inc: { move_count: 1 },
      $set: {
        board: boardAfter,
        turn: nextTurn,
        updated_at: new Date()
      }
    },
    { new: true }
  );
}
// Helper function to build custom layout
export function buildCustomLayout(ruleset: RuleSet): string {
  if (ruleset.startingLayout === 'standard') {
    return buildStandardLayout(ruleset);
  }
  const { boardSize } = ruleset;
  const customLayout = ruleset.startingLayout;
  let board = '';
  // Initialize empty board
  for (let i = 0; i &lt; boardSize * boardSize; i++) {
    board += '#';
  }
  // Place custom pieces
  for (const piece of customLayout.pieces) {
    const index = piece.row * boardSize + piece.col;
    const char = piece.team === 'red'
      ? (piece.type === 'king' ? 'R' : 'r')
      : (piece.type === 'king' ? 'B' : 'b');
    board = board.substring(0, index) + char + board.substring(index + 1);
  }
  // Set light squares to '-'
  for (let row = 0; row &lt; boardSize; row++) {
    for (let col = 0; col &lt; boardSize; col++) {
      if ((row + col) % 2 === 0) {
        const index = row * boardSize + col;
        board = board.substring(0, index) + '-' + board.substring(index + 1);
      }
    }
  }
  return board;
}

ARCHIVO: checkers/apps/web/CLAUDE.md
========================================
Default to using Bun instead of Node.js.
- Use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bun &lt;file&gt;</code> instead of <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">node &lt;file&gt;</code> or <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ts-node &lt;file&gt;</code>
- Use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bun test</code> instead of <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">jest</code> or <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">vitest</code>
- Use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bun build &lt;file.html|file.ts|file.css&gt;</code> instead of <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">webpack</code> or <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">esbuild</code>
- Use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bun install</code> instead of <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">npm install</code> or <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">yarn install</code> or <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">pnpm install</code>
- Use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bun run &lt;script&gt;</code> instead of <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">npm run &lt;script&gt;</code> or <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">yarn run &lt;script&gt;</code> or <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">pnpm run &lt;script&gt;</code>
- Use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bunx &lt;package&gt; &lt;command&gt;</code> instead of <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">npx &lt;package&gt; &lt;command&gt;</code>
- Bun automatically loads .env, so don't use dotenv.
## APIs
- <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Bun.serve()</code> supports WebSockets, HTTPS, and routes. Don't use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">express</code>.
- <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bun:sqlite</code> for SQLite. Don't use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">better-sqlite3</code>.
- <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Bun.redis</code> for Redis. Don't use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ioredis</code>.
- <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Bun.sql</code> for Postgres. Don't use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">pg</code> or <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">postgres.js</code>.
- <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">WebSocket</code> is built-in. Don't use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ws</code>.
- Prefer <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Bun.file</code> over <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">node:fs</code>'s readFile/writeFile
- Bun.$<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ls</code> instead of execa.
## Testing
Use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bun test</code> to run tests.
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ts#index.test.ts
import { test, expect } from "bun:test";
test("hello world", () =&gt; {
  expect(1).toBe(1);
});
</code>
## Frontend
Use HTML imports with <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">Bun.serve()</code>. Don't use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">vite</code>. HTML imports fully support React, CSS, Tailwind.
Server:
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ts#index.ts
import index from "./index.html"
Bun.serve({
  routes: {
    "/": index,
    "/api/users/:id": {
      GET: (req) =&gt; {
        return new Response(JSON.stringify({ id: req.params.id }));
      },
    },
  },
  // optional websocket support
  websocket: {
    open: (ws) =&gt; {
      ws.send("Hello, world!");
    },
    message: (ws, message) =&gt; {
      ws.send(message);
    },
    close: (ws) =&gt; {
      // handle close
    }
  },
  development: {
    hmr: true,
    console: true,
  }
})
</code>
HTML files can import .tsx, .jsx or .js files directly and Bun's bundler will transpile &amp; bundle automatically. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">&lt;link&gt;</code> tags can point to stylesheets and Bun's CSS bundler will bundle.
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">html#index.html
&lt;html&gt;
  &lt;body&gt;
    &lt;h1&gt;Hello, world!&lt;/h1&gt;
    &lt;script type="module" src="./frontend.tsx"&gt;&lt;/script&gt;
  &lt;/body&gt;
&lt;/html&gt;
</code>
With the following <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">frontend.tsx</code>:
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">tsx#frontend.tsx
import React from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
// import .css files directly and it works
import './index.css';
const root = createRoot(document.body);
export default function Frontend() {
  return &lt;h1&gt;Hello, world!&lt;/h1&gt;;
}
root.render(&lt;Frontend /&gt;);
</code>
Then, run index.ts
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">sh
bun --hot ./index.ts
</code>
For more information, read the Bun API docs in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">node_modules/bun-types/docs/**.mdx</code>.

      promotion: false,

    expect(storedMove?.to).toEqual([3, 2]);

    expect(storedMove?.timestamp).toBeInstanceOf(Date);

    expect(updatedGame?.board).toBe(boardAfter);

  it('should increment move_count across multiple moves', async () =&gt; {

    const gameId = game._id.toString();</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    expect(game.move_count).toBe(0);</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">    // First move
