import { test, expect, describe, beforeAll, afterAll, beforeEach, afterEach } from "bun:test"
import { MongoMemoryServer } from 'mongodb-memory-server'
import mongoose from 'mongoose'
import app from '../../src/server/app'
import { Game } from '../../src/models/game.model'
import { PRESET_RULESETS } from '@checkers/shared'
let mongoServer: MongoMemoryServer
let mongoUri: string
beforeAll(async () =&gt; {
  mongoServer = await MongoMemoryServer.create()
  mongoUri = mongoServer.getUri()</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  // Connect to the in-memory MongoDB
  await mongoose.connect(mongoUri)
})
afterAll(async () =&gt; {
  // Clean up
  await mongoose.disconnect()
  await mongoServer.stop()
})
beforeEach(async () =&gt; {
  // Clear the database before each test
  await Game.deleteMany({})
})
describe('Game API', () =&gt; {
  describe('POST /api/game', () =&gt; {
    test('should create a new game with preset ruleset', async () =&gt; {
      const response = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ruleset: { preset: 'english' },
          mode: 'pvp'
        })
      })
      expect(response.status).toBe(201)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('gameId')
      expect(data.state).toHaveProperty('status')
      expect(data.state.status).toBe('active')
      expect(data.state.ruleset).toEqual(PRESET_RULESETS.english)
      expect(data.state.mode).toBe('pvp')
      expect(data.state.turn).toBe('red')
    })
    test('should create a new game with custom ruleset', async () =&gt; {
      const customRuleset = {
        name: 'Custom Rules',
        boardSize: 8,
        normalMoveDirections: 'forward',
        kingMoveDistance: 'flying',
        captureRequired: true,
        captureMaximum: false,
        captureBackward: false,
        promotionEndsJump: true,
        startingLayout: 'standard'
      }
      const response = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ruleset: customRuleset,
          mode: 'pva',
          difficulty: 'medium',
          ai_team: 'black',
          algorithm: 'minimax'
        })
      })
      expect(response.status).toBe(201)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('gameId')
      expect(data.state).toHaveProperty('status')
      expect(data.state.status).toBe('active')
      expect(data.state.ruleset).toEqual(customRuleset)
      expect(data.state.mode).toBe('pva')
      expect(data.state.turn).toBe('red')
    })
    test('should return 400 for invalid request body', async () =&gt; {
      const response = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({})
      })
      expect(response.status).toBe(400)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('error')
    })
    test('should return 400 for invalid ruleset', async () =&gt; {
      const response = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ruleset: { preset: 'invalid' },
          mode: 'pvp'
        })
      })
      expect(response.status).toBe(400)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('error')
    })
  })
  describe('GET /api/game/:gameId/state', () =&gt; {
    test('should return game state for valid game', async () =&gt; {
      // First create a game
      const createResponse = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ruleset: { preset: 'english' },
          mode: 'pvp'
        })
      })
      const createData = await createResponse.json()
      const gameId = createData.gameId
      // Now get the game state
      const response = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${gameId}/state</code>)
      expect(response.status).toBe(200)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('ruleset')
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('mode')
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('board')
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('turn')
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('status')
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('move_count')
    })
    test('should return 404 for non-existent game', async () =&gt; {
      const fakeGameId = '000000000000000000000000'
      const response = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${fakeGameId}/state</code>)
      expect(response.status).toBe(404)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data.error).toBe('Game not found')
    })
    test('should return 400 for invalid game ID format', async () =&gt; {
      const invalidGameId = 'invalid-id'
      const response = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${invalidGameId}/state</code>)
      expect(response.status).toBe(400)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data.error).toBe('Invalid game ID format')
    })
  })
  describe('GET /api/game/:gameId/legal', () =&gt; {
    test('should return legal moves for current player', async () =&gt; {
      // Create a game
      const createResponse = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ruleset: { preset: 'english' },
          mode: 'pvp'
        })
      })
      const createData = await createResponse.json()
      const gameId = createData.gameId
      // Get legal moves
      const response = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${gameId}/legal?row=2&amp;col=1</code>)
      expect(response.status).toBe(200)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('turn')
      expect(data).toHaveProperty('legal_moves')
      expect(Array.isArray(data.legal_moves)).toBe(true)
      expect(data.turn).toBe('red') // Red goes first
    })
    test('should return 404 for non-existent game', async () =&gt; {
      const fakeGameId = '000000000000000000000000'
      const response = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${fakeGameId}/legal</code>)
      expect(response.status).toBe(404)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data.error).toBe('Game not found')
    })
  })
  describe('POST /api/game/:gameId/move', () =&gt; {
    test('should make a valid move and update game state', async () =&gt; {
      // Create a game
      const createResponse = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ruleset: { preset: 'english' },
          mode: 'pvp'
        })
      })
      const createData = await createResponse.json()
      const gameId = createData.gameId
      // Get legal moves to find a valid move
      const legalResponse = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${gameId}/legal?row=2&amp;col=1</code>)
      const legalData = await legalResponse.json()
      if (legalData.legal_moves.length === 0) {
        test.skip('No legal moves available for this game state')
        return
      }
      const validMove = legalData.legal_moves[0]
      // Make the move
      const moveResponse = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${gameId}/move</code>, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          from: validMove.from,
          to: validMove.to,
          captures: validMove.captures || [],
          promotion: validMove.promotion || false
        })
      })
      expect(moveResponse.status).toBe(200)
      const moveData = await moveResponse.json()
      expect(moveData.state).toBeDefined()
      expect(moveData.state.status).toBe('active')
      expect(moveData.state.board).toBeDefined()
      expect(moveData.state.turn).toBeDefined()
      expect(moveData.state.move_count).toBeDefined()
    })
    test('should return 404 for non-existent game', async () =&gt; {
      const fakeGameId = '000000000000000000000000'
      const response = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${fakeGameId}/move</code>, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          from: [2, 1],
          to: [3, 2],
          captures: [],
          promotion: false
        })
      })
      expect(response.status).toBe(404)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data.error).toBe('Game not found')
    })
    test('should return 400 for game that is already over', async () =&gt; {
      // Create a game
      const createResponse = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ruleset: { preset: 'english' },
          mode: 'pvp'
        })
      })
      const createData = await createResponse.json()
      const gameId = createData.gameId
      // Manually update game to be over
      await Game.findByIdAndUpdate(gameId, { status: 'red_wins' })
      // Try to make a move
      const response = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${gameId}/move</code>, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          from: [2, 1],
          to: [3, 2],
          captures: [],
          promotion: false
        })
      })
      expect(response.status).toBe(409)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data.error).toBe('Game is already over')
    })
    test('should return 400 for invalid move format', async () =&gt; {
      // Create a game
      const createResponse = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ruleset: { preset: 'english' },
          mode: 'pvp'
        })
      })
      const createData = await createResponse.json()
      const gameId = createData.gameId
      // Try to make an invalid move
      const response = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${gameId}/move</code>, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          from: [2, 1],
          to: [3, 2]
          // Missing captures and promotion fields
        })
      })
      expect(response.status).toBe(400)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data.error).toBe('Invalid move format')
    })
    test('should return 400 for wrong player turn', async () =&gt; {
      // Create a game
      const createResponse = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ruleset: { preset: 'english' },
          mode: 'pvp'
        })
      })
      const createData = await createResponse.json()
      const gameId = createData.gameId
      // Try to move a black piece when it's red's turn
      const response = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${gameId}/move</code>, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          from: [5, 0], // Black piece position
          to: [4, 1],
          captures: [],
          promotion: false
        })
      })
      expect(response.status).toBe(400)
      const data = await response.json()
      expect(data.error).toBe('It is not your turn to move this piece')
    })
    test('should return 400 for illegal move', async () =&gt; {
      // Create a game
      const createResponse = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ruleset: { preset: 'english' },
          mode: 'pvp'
        })
      })
      const createData = await createResponse.json()
      const gameId = createData.gameId
      // Try to make an illegal move (moving diagonally forward 2 spaces)
      const response = await app.request(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">/api/game/${gameId}/move</code>, {

          mode: 'pvp'
        })

        method: 'POST',

    })
    test('should return 400 for game that is already over', async () =&gt; {
      // Create a game
      const createResponse = await app.request('/api/game', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },

          mode: 'pvp'

        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ team: 'black' })
      })

    test('should return 400 for invalid team', async () =&gt; {

/**
 * index.ts — Hono server entry point for the web application.
 * 
 * This is the main entry point that starts the Hono server and establishes
 * the MongoDB connection using environment variables for configuration.
 */
import { Hono } from "hono";
import { cors } from "hono/cors";
import mongoose from "mongoose";
// ─── Environment Configuration ──────────────────────────────────────────────
const PORT = parseInt(process.env.PORT || "3000", 10);
const MONGODB_URI = process.env.MONGODB_URI || "mongodb://localhost:27017/checkers";
const ALLOWED_ORIGIN = process.env.ALLOWED_ORIGIN || "*";
// ─── MongoDB Connection ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function connectToMongoDB(): Promise&lt;void&gt; {
  await mongoose.connect(MONGODB_URI);
  console.log("✅ MongoDB connected successfully");
}
// ─── Hono App Setup ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const app = new Hono();
// CORS middleware restricting to ALLOWED_ORIGIN
app.use("*", cors({ origin: ALLOWED_ORIGIN }));
// Global error handler
app.onError((err, c) =&gt; {
  console.error("Unhandled error:", err);
  return c.json({ error: "Internal server error" }, 500);
});
// ─── Health Check Endpoint ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
app.get("/health", (c) =&gt; {
  return c.json({ status: "ok", service: "web" });
});
// ─── Server Startup ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function startServer(): Promise&lt;void&gt; {
  // Connect to MongoDB
  await connectToMongoDB();
  // Start the Hono server
  console.log(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">🚀 Web server starting on port ${PORT}</code>);</p><p class="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words">  Bun.serve({
    fetch: app.fetch,
    port: PORT
  }, () =&gt; {
    console.log(<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">🌐 Server running at http://localhost:${PORT}</code>);
  });
}
// Start the server
startServer().catch((error) =&gt; {
  console.error("Failed to start server:", error);
  process.exit(1);
});

# web
To install dependencies:
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bash
bun install
</code>
To run:
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">bash
bun run index.ts
</code>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>4. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ava</code> mode doesn't trigger AI on game creation</strong>
The prompt spec states: <em>"If mode is 'ava', after creating the game, immediately trigger the first AI move for the red team before responding."</em> The <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">POST /</code> route handler creates the game and returns immediately for all modes. The first AI move in ava mode is never fired.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>5. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">createGame</code> for pva in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">game.service.test.ts</code> is missing <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">algorithm</code></strong></p>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>🟡 Moderate Issues</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>6. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ai.client.test.ts</code> still uses wrong <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> fields</strong>
This was flagged in the previous review and not fixed. The mock ruleset uses <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">kingsCanMoveBackwards</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">maxConsecutiveJumps</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">forceCapture</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">forceCaptureMax</code> — none of which exist in the actual <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">RuleSet</code> type. The correct fields are <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">normalMoveDirections</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">kingMoveDistance</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">captureRequired</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">captureMaximum</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">captureBackward</code>, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">promotionEndsJump</code>. Fix the mock to use <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ENGLISH</code> from <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">@checkers/shared</code> directly.</p>

<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>8. Missing "AI turn" 409 in pva move route</strong>
The prompt spec says: <em>"If mode is 'pva' and game.turn equals game.ai_team, return 409: 'It is the AI's turn'."</em> Currently the route only checks if the piece belongs to the current turn and returns a generic 400. A player attempting to submit a move on the AI's turn should get a specific 409 with that message.</p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>9. <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">.ts</code> extension in import</strong>
<code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">ai.client.ts</code> line 2: <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">import { addMoveToHistory, updateGame } from '../models/game.service.ts'</code> — the <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">.ts</code> extension should be omitted: <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">'../models/game.service'</code>.</p>
<hr class="border-border-200 border-t-0.5 my-3 mx-1.5">
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>🟡 Still Missing from Tests</strong></p>
<p class="font-claude-response-body break-words whitespace-normal leading-[1.7]"><strong>10. No AI service mock for pva tests in <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">game.api.test.ts</code></strong>
The "create game with custom ruleset" test creates a <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">pva</code> game. When that game later has moves made against it in other tests, <code class="bg-text-200/5 border border-0.5 border-border-300 text-danger-000 whitespace-pre-wrap rounded-[0.4rem] px-1 py-px text-[0.9rem]">requestAiMove</code> will attempt a real HTTP call to port 4000. Mock it globally for the test file:</p>
